Let's think of iterative algorithm "Critical Point Search":
1. Start a.
2. Compute b, M.
3. Set λ_k = exp((b_k - M)/τ) / sum (or top m). This weights all slightly, symmetric.
4. Solve Tλ x = 1 (least squares) for x. Normalize x≥0 to sum 1. Let a_new.
5. Rescale/line search direction between a and a_new: a_t=(1-t)a + t a_new; if exact objective improves, adopt, else reduce t.
Repeat, decreasing τ.
This replaces gradient descent with a linear solve that enforces weighted stationarity. It might jump to better basins.

CG solve details:
```
def solve_T_lambda_one(lambda, n, niter=80, mu=1e-6):
    T1 = apply_T(ones, lambda)
    rhs = T1 + maybe? Actually bA=T1.
    x = np.ones(n)/n
    r = rhs - (T(T x)+mu x)
    p = r
    rsold = dot(r,r)
    for it:
       Ap = T(T p)+mu p
       alpha = rsold / dot(p,Ap)
       x += alpha p
       r -= alpha Ap
       rsnew=...
       beta=rsnew/rsold
       p = r + beta p
       if ... break
    x=np.maximum(x,0); s=x.sum(); if s>0 x/=s
    return x
```

Given f normalized sum=√(2n)? They maximize sum g subject to ||f*g||∞≤M, where M=||f*f||∞. Then rescale g to normalization and convex update f←(1-t)f+tg. Primal optimum g satisfies? Complementary: μ_k((f*g)_k - M)=0, so y only where (f*g)=M. Then A^T y_i =? stationarity for primal: sum μ (f*g? derivative?) Actually primal variables g, constraints matrix B where (B g)_k = ∑_i f_i g_{k-i} = (f*g)_k. Derivative of constraint wrt g_j = f_{k-j}. KKT: 1 = B^T μ, where B symmetric? B^T μ_j =∑_k μ_k f_{k-j} = Tμ f_j (same T). So Tμ f = const (1). Thus if we find μ satisfying Tμ f =1, then it's stationary. The dual LP with ≥ may yield equality if f_j>0. This is exactly our λ condition! And primal optimum g provides a sequence in feasible set of constraints with f. The SOTA update f←g. So solving dual LP for μ and primal g is the same KKT fixed point. This may be more principled.

Can we solve the dual LP approximately? It is a covering LP: min ∑y s.t. T y ≥ 1 (with T y = (A^T y), nonnegative), y≥0. The T matrix is n x 2n-1 (T_{i,k}=a_{k-i} if valid). Primal LP in scipy/cvxpy:
Variables g length n ≥0.
Constraints: for k=0..2n-2, ∑_{j} a_{k-j} g_j ≤1. This matrix B: B[k,j]=a_{k-j} if 0≤k-j<n else 0. It is banded Toeplitz (actually each row has up to n nonzeros; total n( n? ) ~ n²/2). It can be represented as a Toeplitz/Hankel? For convexity, need dense or sparse. We can build with scipy.sparse Toeplitz? CVXPY can accept `cp.matmul`? We need precompute matrix? Could use `cvxpy.Constant` with a LinearOperator? CVXPY requires matrices. Could build sparse matrix efficiently using `scipy.sparse.diags`? B is Haar (convolution) with variable; can construct as banded with diagonals: For each lag l = j - k? B_{k,j}=a_{k-j}. Let d=j-k. Then entries determined by a_{-d}. For d from -(n-1) to n-1. def outer_balance(a, max_time=60):
    a = np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return a
    a_n, S = normalize_max(a)  # max conv =1
    best_a=a_n; best_S=S; best_score=2*n/best_S^2? Since max=1, score=2n/S^2.
    deadline = time.monotonic()+max_time
    t = 1.0
    while time < deadline-10:
        g = balance_inner(a_best, time_per_inner, n)
        # candidate g normalized max
        g_max, S_g = normalize_max(g)
        # line search t in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0]
        cands = [best_a]
        for tau in (1.0,0.75,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1): cands.append(normalize_max((1-tau)*best_a + tau*g_max)[0])
        Evaluate exact score via S (since max normalized) no need conv? Need S after normalization.
        choose best_S > current + 1e-10.
        if not: break or reduce inner time
        # maybe if g close to a (max abs diff small) and S improvement tiny break
    return best_a (max-normalized)
```

def inner_value(g):
   n=G_INNER_N; N=G_INNER_N; fh=G_INNER_FHAT
   gh=np.fft.rfft(g,N)
   b=np.fft.irfft(fh*gh,N, n=2*n-1)

For grad:
```
def inner_grad(v):
    g=softmax(v)
    b=...
    M=b.max(); tau=G_INNER_TAU
    u=(b-M)/tau; u=np.clip(u,-100,0); ew=np.exp(u); logsum=np.log(ew.sum())
    val = M + tau*logsum (for obj)
    w=ew/logsum; w=np.maximum(w,0)
    # h = T_w f
    wr=np.flip(w)
    wh=np.fft.rfft(wr,N)
    h=np.fft.irfft(fh*wh,N)
    h = h[G_INNER_S - np.arange(n)]  # shape n
    grad_h = h
    dot = np.dot(g, grad_h)
    grad = g*(grad_h - dot)
    return grad
```

Soft objective τ log∑exp((b-M)/τ)+M. Gradient of this with respect to g: ∑ w_k ∂b_k/∂g_j =∑w_k f_{k-j}=h_j. Good.

```
def inner_obj(v):
    if G_INNER_F is None: return np.inf
    g=softmax(v)
    n=G_INNER_N; N=G_INNER_NFFT; fh=G_INNER_FHAT
    gh=np.fft.rfft(g,N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fh*gh,N, n=2*n-1)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    M=float(b.max())
    tau=G_INNER_TAU
    if tau<=0 or M<=0: return M
    u=(b-M)/tau
    # to avoid overflow, u<=0
    u=np.clip(u,-100.0,0.0)
    ew=np.exp(u)
    logsum=float(np.log(ew.sum()))
    return float(M + tau*logsum)
```

Now, subgradient polish for original sum=1:
```
def polish_subgradient(a, max_time):
    a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); a/=s
    best_score=score_fft(a); deadline...
    step=0.03; top=200
    while time < deadline:
       b=conv_square(a); M=b.max(); if M<=0 break
       # active: indices where b >= M - rel*M? Use top max
       top_count=min(500,len(b))
       if top_count < len(b): idx=np.argpartition(b, -top_count)[-top_count:]
       else idx=np.arange(len(b))
       w=np.zeros(len(b)); w[idx]=1; w/=w.sum()
       # sym? if score objective max symmetric b; use sym to reduce hash maybe: w=(w+w[::-1])/2
       h=apply_T(w,a,N,fa, S, idx_arr)
       grad_a=2*h; dot=np.dot(a,grad_a)
       direction = a*(grad_a-dot)
       # line search in log-space
       v=np.log(a+1e-300)
       best_local=a; best_ls=best_score
       for alpha in [step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.1, step*0.05]:
           v_new=v - alpha*direction
           a_new=softmax(v_new)
           sc=score_fft(a_new)
           if sc<best_ls-1e-12: accept; break
       if not accept: step*=0.7
       else: step=min(0.1, step*1.02)
       if step<1e-5: break
```
This may be slow if expensive score each candidate (FFT conv_square). But top small and time maybe 60s. Use exact subgrad only final 60-120s.

`softmax(x)`:
```
def softmax(x):
    m=np.max(x)
    e=np.exp(x-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.ones(len(x), dtype=float)/len(x)
    return e/s
```

`next_pow2(n)`:
```
if n<=1: return 1
return 1 << (int(n)-1).bit_length()
```

```
n=len(a)
if n<1: return np.array([])

   return np.maximum(np.convolve(a,a),0)
N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N,n=2*n-1)
b=np.maximum(b,0)
return b
```

`consider_sequence(seq, tracker)`:
```
def consider_sequence(seq, tracker):
   seq=np.asarray(seq,float)
   seq=seq[np.isfinite(seq)]
   if len(seq)==0: return
   seq=np.maximum(seq,0)
   s=seq.sum()
   if s<1e-12: return
   seq=seq/s
   score=score_from_a(seq)
   if score < tracker[0] - 1e-12:
       # optional exact check if len<=12000 and tracker[1] is not None
       try:
          exact=evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist())
       except Exception:
          exact=score
       if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < tracker[0]-1e-12:
          tracker[0]=float(exact); tracker[1]=seq.copy()
       elif np.isnotfinite(tracker[0]): # first
          tracker[0]=score; tracker[1]=seq.copy()
       else:
          # if internal score better but exact not, maybe store? To be safe use exact only if finite, else internal
          if not np.isfinite(tracker[0]):
             tracker[0]=score; tracker[1]=seq.copy()
```

Simpler: call exact only when candidate beats threshold e.g., tracker[0]-1e-6 and time? The system will evaluate final anyway. To avoid memory, we can do exact at end for top candidates. g[0]=L.
For k≥1:
2 L g_k + ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} g_i g_{k-i} = 1.  (since c_k=1)
Thus g_k = (1 - ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} g_i g_{k-i})/(2L).
This is O(n²) if naive. But n=4806, O(23M) per L. We can compute for a handful L in Python? 23M multiplications in Python loop too slow. We can vectorize using FFT? Each dot C but overhead 5000, maybe 0.1-0.5s. Feasible for few L. Let's implement efficient enough:
```
def flat_root_profile(n, L):
    g=np.zeros(n); g[0]=L
    for k in range(1,n):
        # sum_{i=1}^{k-1} g[i]*g[k-i]
        if k>1:
            s = np.dot(g[1:k], g[k-1:0:-1])  # length k-1
        else: s=0
        gk=(1.0-s)/(2.0*L)
        if not finite or gk < -1e-9: break? set 0?
        g[k]=max(0,gk)
    return g
```

Let's write each in detail.

`conv_square`:
```
def conv_square(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 1500:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        return np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    N = next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N, n=2*n - 1)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
For n=1500, np.convolve O(2.25M) okay. For many score calls, maybe always FFT faster? FFT length 4096; O(4096 log) ~50k, faster. But np.convolve C double maybe 2M multiply ~0.0005s; FFT maybe similar. Use FFT for n>800 maybe. Set threshold 800. Exactness okay.

`score_from_a` can use convolution. It normalizes and returns. For score during optimization, avoid exact `np.convolve` if FFT. Good.

`normalize_max_and_sum`: If a all zeros return None,0. Use `conv_square` which normalizes? It doesn't. First sum-normalize to avoid huge M if scale arbitrary. Good.

`flat_root_profile` with negative handling:
```
def flat_root_profile(n, L):
    g=np.zeros(n)
    if L<=0: L=1e-6
    g[0]=L
    for k in range(1,n):
        if k == 1:
            s=0.0
        else:
            s=float(np.dot(g[1:k], g[k-1:0:-1]))
        val=(1.0-s)/(2.0*L)
        if not np.isfinite(val) or val < -1e-8:
            # set 0 and maybe continue? To preserve length, set 0
            val=0.0
        g[k]=max(0.0,val)
    # If too many zeros? return
    return g
```
But if val becomes negative and we set 0, later recurrence no longer meaningful; s computations with zeros may get positive again? Could be. Maybe break and assign exponential tail? Simpler: if negative beyond small, set 0 and continue; profile may become zero tail. For starts, not critical. But if many zeros, bad. We can stop and fill remaining with small B tail? Use:
```
for k ... if val< -1e-9:
    for j in range(k,n): g[j]=1e-12
    break

def row_solve_refine(a, max_time):
   deadline=time+max_time
   f,S=normalize_max_and_sum(a)
   best_f,best_S = f,S
   # tau factors
   while time<deadline-2:
      improved=False
      for tau_factor in [0.3,0.15,0.07,0.03]:
         if time>deadline: break
         b=conv_square(best_f); M=b.max(); if M<=0: break
         w=make_lambda_soft(b, tau_factor*M)
         cand=solve_T_lambda_one(w, len(best_f), iters=60)
         c,Sc=normalize_max_and_sum(cand)
         # line search with best_f
         for t in [1,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.1]:
            cc=best_f*(1-t)+c*t; _,Scc=normalize_max_and_sum(cc)
            if Scc>best_S: best_f=cc/best_norm?; improved=True
         if improved: break
      if not improved: break
   return best_f/best_S
```

CG details for solve:
```
def solve_T_lambda_one(w, n, iters=50, mu_frac=1e-5):
    w = np.asarray(w); L=len(w); S=2*n-2; idx=np.arange(S, S-n, -1)
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    w_rev_hat = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(w), N)
    ones=np.ones(n)
    b = apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat,N,idx,ones)
    normb=np.max(np.abs(b))
    if normb<=0: return ones/n
    mu = max(1e-12, mu_frac*normb)
    x=np.ones(n)/n
    # define inline matvec function? no nested; write a top-level `T_apply` and call.
    # r = b - (T(T x)+ mu x)
    y=apply_T_generic(...,x); Ty=apply_T_generic(...,y); r=b-(Ty+mu*x)
    p=r.copy(); rsold=dot(r,r)
    for it in range(iters):
       y=apply_T_generic(...,p); Ty=apply_T_generic(...,y); Ap=Ty+mu*p
       pAp=dot(p,Ap)
       if pAp <=1e-300: break
       alpha=rsold/pAp
       x += alpha*p
       r -= alpha*Ap
       rsnew=dot(r,r)
       if np.sqrt(rsnew) < 1e-9*normb: break
       p = r + (rsnew/rsold)*p
       rsold=rsnew
    x=np.maximum(x,0)
    s=x.sum(); if s<=0: return ones/n
    return x/s
```
Since T may be large, b norm maybe ~n*avg w *? For w distributed length 2n, w sum1; T1_i could be up to 1. Good. μ small. CG may produce negative? We project.
Could use `iters=30` for speed.

`polish_subgradient`:
```
def polish_subgradient(a, max_time):
   a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum(); best=score_from_a(a); deadline...
   step=0.05; N=next_pow2(3n-2); S=2n-2; idx=...
   for step_cycle in ...:
      while time<deadline:
        b=conv_square(a); M=b.max(); if M<=0 break
        top=min(300, len(b)); idx_b=np.argpartition(b,-top)[-top:]
        w=np.zeros(len(b)); w[idx_b]=1.0; w/=w.sum(); w=0.5*(w+w[::-1]); w/=w.sum()
        wh=rfft(w,N); fa=rfft(a,N); h=irfft(fa*wh,N); h=h[idx]
        grad_a=2*h; dot=dot(a,grad_a); dir=a*(grad_a-dot)
        v=np.log(a+1e-300)
        improved=False
        for alpha in [step, step/2, step/4, step/8]:
           v_new=v-alpha*dir; a_new=softmax(v_new); sc=score_from_a(a_new)
           if sc<best-1e-12: a=a_new; best=sc; improved=True; break
        if not improved: step*=0.7
        else: step=min(0.2, step*1.05)
        if step<1e-5: break
   return a
```
This may be expensive; use limited 60s.

In polished subgradient, applying h requires `fa`; each step compute conv square and rfft. Fine.

`inner_obj`:
```
def inner_obj(v):
    try:
        n=G_INNER_N
        if n <= 0 or G_INNER_FHAT is None:
            return float('inf')
        g=softmax(v)
        N=G_INNER_NFFT
        gh=np.fft.rfft(g,N)
        b=np.fft.irfft(G_INNER_FHAT*gh,N,n=2*n-1)
        b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
        M=float(b.max())
        tau=G_INNER_TAU
        if tau <= 1e-300 or M <= 0.0:
            return M
        u=(b-M)/tau
        u=np.clip(u,-100.0,0.0)
        ew=np.exp(u)
        s=sum
        return float(M + tau*np.log(s))
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
```

def finish_candidates(tracker):
    best=tracker[1]
    candidates=[]
    if best is not None:
       a=np.maximum(best,0); a=a/a.sum(); candidates.append(a)
       # add tiny positive floor
       b=a+1e-15; b=b/b.sum(); candidates.append(b)
       # baseline if global
    if 'height_sequence_1' in globals(): add normalized
    best_seq=None; best_exact=inf
    for c in candidates:
       try:
          sc=evaluate_sequence(c.tolist())
       except Exception:
          sc=score_from_a(c)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_exact:
          best_exact=sc; best_seq=c
    if best_seq is None: best_seq=best
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```

```
def trim_edges(a, eps):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); a/=s
   if len(a)==0: return a
   mx=a.max(); thresh=eps*mx
   l=0; r=len(a)-1
   while l<r and a[l] < thresh: l+=1
   while r>l and a[r] < thresh: r-=1
   return a[l:r+1]
```

def direct_lbfgs(a, max_time, global_deadline):
   a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum(); n=len(a)
   score=score_from_a(a); best=a
   if not finite score: return a
   N=next_pow2(3*n-2); S=2n-2; idx; set globals
   v=np.log(a+1e-300)
   deadline=...
   factors=[0.3,0.15,0.07,0.03,0.015,0.008]
   base_iters=...
   for factor...:
     M=score/(2*n)
     tau=factor*max(M,1e-12)
     G_ORIG_TAU=tau
     G_DEADLINE=deadline
     maxiter=...
     res=minimize(orig_obj,v,jac=orig_grad, ...)
     if success: v=res.x; a=softmax(v)
     sc=score_from_a(a); if sc<score: score=sc; best=a
     if time... break
   return best
```

```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    budget = min(2400.0, float(budget_s)) if finite else 2400
    global_deadline = time.monotonic() + budget - 90
    tracker=[inf,None,0]
    # baseline
    try globals...
    if len>0: consider_sequence(normalize)
    if tracker[1] is None:
       n_base=4096; consider_sequence(profile_B(n_base), tracker)
    else: n_base=len(tracker[1])
    print
    # list of (label, seq, budget, allow_direct)
    plans=[]
    if time < deadline-300:
       plans.append(('base', tracker[1].copy(), 500, True))
       b=profile_B(n_base); plans.append(('B',b,250,True))
       if n_base<=6000:
          for L in [0.85,0.75]: plans.append(('flat', flat_root_profile(n_base,L), 200, True))
       plans.append(('twoeps', two_endpoint_profile(n_base,0.5), 150, False))
       # perturb best
       pert=perturb_sequence(tracker[1], 0.1, 123); plans.append(('pert',pert,200,False))
    # other lengths after baseline maybe dynamic using current best
    other_lengths=[]
    for n in [8192,12288]:
       if n != n_base and n <= 14000 and n>=1024: other_lengths.append(n)
    for n in other_lengths:
       if time.monotonic() > global_deadline-300: break
       starts=[]
       starts.append(interpolate_sequence(tracker[1],n))
       starts.append(profile_B(n))
       # maybe twoeps
       for st in starts:
          plans.append((f'len{n}', st, 250 if n<10000 else 180, False))
    # execute plans
    for label, seq, bud, allow in plans:
       if time.monotonic() > global_deadline - 120: break
       # maybe reduce budget if time
       run_start(seq, min(bud, max(30,(global_deadline-time)*0.5)), tracker, global_deadline, allow_direct=allow)
       print
    # final polish on best
    if tracker[1] is not None and time < global_deadline:
       a=tracker[1].copy()
       # direct final
       rem = global_deadline - time.monotonic()
       direct_time = min(300, rem*0.5, 300)
       if direct_time>40 and len(a)<=14000:
           a2=direct_lbfgs(a, direct_time, global_deadline); consider_sequence(a2,tracker)
       # row solve
       row_time=min(120, (global_deadline-time)*0.5)
       if row_time>20: a2=row_solve_refine(tracker[1], row_time, global_deadline); consider
       # trim/polish
       pol_time=min(120, global_deadline-time)
       if pol_time>10: a2=polish_subgradient(tracker[1], pol_time, global_deadline); consider
       # trimmed candidates
       a2=trim_edges(tracker[1],1e-7); consider
       a2=trim_edges(tracker[1],1e-6); consider
    return finalize(tracker)
```

```
def perturb_sequence(a, sigma, seed):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   v=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   v += rng.normal(0,sigma,len(v))
   return softmax(v)
```

i=np.arange(n)

`run_start` top-level:
```
def run_start(a, budget, tracker, global_deadline, allow_direct=True):
    start_time=time
    if a is None or len(a)==0: return
    n=len(a)
    if n<8 or n>16000: return
    # Outer
    rem=global_deadline-time
    outer_budget=int(min(budget*0.6, max(20, min(600, rem)*0.5)))
    if outer_budget>15:
       b=outer_balance(a, outer_budget, global_deadline)
       if b is not None:
          consider_sequence(b, tracker)
          a = tracker[1] if tracker[1] is not None else a
    # row refine
    if allow_direct: # maybe also apply to large
       row_budget=int(min(budget*0.15, 60, (global_deadline-time)*0.5))
       if row_budget>15 and len(a)<=15000:
          b=row_solve_refine(a,row_budget,global_deadline)
          consider; a=tracker...
    # direct
    if allow_direct:
       direct_budget=int(min(budget*0.25, 300, (global_deadline-time)*0.5))
       if len(a)<=12000 and direct_budget>30:
          b=direct_lbfgs(a,direct_budget,global_deadline)
          consider
    # polish
    pol_budget=int(min(budget*0.1, 60, (global_deadline-time)*0.5))
    if pol_budget>10 and len(a)<=15000:
       b=polish_subgradient(a,pol_budget,global_deadline); consider
```

`softmax`:
```
def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
         return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64)/x.size
    return e/s
```
If x all -inf? max finite? If logs of zeros? We initialize with eps, so finite. If x has nan, return uniform? Add check:
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
    x=np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-1e6)
```

`next_pow2`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`conv_square`:
```
def conv_square(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 800:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N,n=2*n-1)
    c=np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[c<0]=0.0
    return c
```
For n=0 okay.

def score_from_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=a[np.isfinite(a)]; a=np.maximum(a,0)
    n=a.size

    s=a.sum()

    b=conv_square(a)
    if b.size==0: return inf

```

So after normalize, use `2.0*n*M`. Be careful. In function above, after `a=a/s`, score should be 2n*M. If we use s old, wrong. Let's implement:

`profile_B`:
```
def profile_B(n):
    n=int(n)
    if n<=0: return np.array([],float)
    a=np.ones(n,float)
    for i in range(1,n):
       a[i]=a[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
    return a
```

`trim_edges`:
```
def trim_edges(a, eps):
    a=... normalize
    if a.size<=10: return a
    mx=a.max(); thresh=max(eps*mx, 10*np.finfo(float).tiny)
    l=0; r=a.size-1
    while l<r and a[l] < thresh: l+=1
    while r>l and a[r] < thresh: r-=1
    b=a[l:r+1]; s=b.sum(); if s>0: b/=s
    return b
```

Apply helpers:

def apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat, N, idx, x):
    xh=np.fft.rfft(x,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(w_rev_hat*xh,N)
    return c[idx].copy()
```

`solve_T_lambda_one(w,n,iters=40,mu_frac=1e-5)`:
```
def solve_T_lambda_one(w, n, iters=40, mu_frac=1e-5):
    w=np.asarray(w,float)
    if w.size != 2*n-1:
       # pad/clip?
    L=2*n-1; S=L-1; idx=S-np.arange(n)
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    w_rev_hat=np.fft.rfft(np.flip(w),N)
    ones=np.ones(n)
    b=apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat,N,idx,ones)
    normb=float(np.max(np.abs(b)))
    if normb<=0: return ones/n
    mu=max(1e-12, mu_frac*normb)
    x=ones/n
    # helper inline? define top-level function `Tm`? Need Ap = T(Tp)+mu p. Use apply_T_generic.
    y=apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat,N,idx,x); Ty=apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat,N,idx,y); r=b-(Ty+mu*x)
    p=r.copy(); rsold=float(np.dot(r,r))
    for _ in range(iters):
       y=apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat,N,idx,p); Ty=apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat,N,idx,y); Ap=Ty+mu*p
       pAp=float(np.dot(p,Ap))
       if not np.isfinite(pAp) or pAp <= 1e-300: break
       alpha=rsold/pAp
       x=x+alpha*p
       r=r-alpha*Ap
       rsnew=float(np.dot(r,r))
       if not np.isfinite(rsnew) or rsnew < 1e-18*normb*normb: break
       if rsold <= 1e-300: break
       p = r + (rsnew/rsold)*p
       rsold=rsnew
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    s=float(x.sum())
    if s<=0: return ones/n
    return x/s
```
If w sum not 1, b scaled; okay.

`make_lambda_soft(b, tau, sym=True)`:
```
def make_lambda_soft(b, tau, sym=True):
    M=float(b.max())
    if tau<=0 or M<=0: return np.zeros_like(b)
    u=(b-M)/tau; u=np.clip(u,-100,0)
    w=np.exp(u); s=w.sum(); if s<=0: return np.zeros_like(b); w/=s
    if sym:
       w=0.5*(w+w[::-1])
       s=w.sum(); if s>0: w/=s
    return w
```
For original b symmetric; for row solve symmetric. For inner LSE not use.

def balance_inner(f, max_time, global_deadline, warm_g=None):
    f=np.asarray(f,float); f=f[np.isfinite(f)]; f=np.maximum(f,0)
    s=f.sum(); if s<=0: return None
    # f should be maxnorm; but if not, normalize? If caller passes maxnorm. To be safe, normalize max if max f*f >>1? But that would change scale and g? g independent; but tau scale. We can normalize max to 1: _,_ = normalize_max? but cost. Do if max(f) >2 or min? Simpler assume.
    n=f.size; deadline=min(time.monotonic()+max_time, global_deadline)
    if n<2: return f/s
    N=next_pow2(3*n-2); S=2*n-2; idx=S-np.arange(n)
    fh=np.fft.rfft(f,N)
    G_INNER_N=n; G_INNER_NFFT=N; G_INNER_IDX=idx; G_INNER_FHAT=fh; G_INNER_S=S
    if warm_g is not None and len(warm_g)==n:
         g=np.maximum(warm_g,0); sg=g.sum(); if sg>0: g=g/sg
         else: g=f/s
    else:
         g=f/s; sg=f.sum(); g/=sg
    v=np.log(g+1e-300)
    best_g=g.copy()
    best_val=inner_exact_global(g)
    factors=[0.30,0.15,0.07,0.03,0.015]
    base=maxiter_for(n, max_time*len(factors))
    for factor in factors:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline-0.5: break
        if best_val <=0: break
        tau=factor*max(best_val,1e-12)
        G_INNER_TAU=tau; G_DEADLINE=deadline
        maxiter=max(20, base//len(factors))
        try:
            res=minimize(inner_obj, v, jac=inner_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxiter*3,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':10,'disp':False}, callback=stop_callback)
            if hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                v=res.x
        except Exception:
            pass
        g=softmax(v)
        val=inner_exact_global(g)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
             best_val=val; best_g=g.copy()
    return best_g
```

`outer_balance`:
```
def outer_balance(a, max_time, global_deadline):
    nm, S = normalize_max_and_sum(a)
    if nm is None: return None
    best_f=nm.copy(); best_S=S
    warm=None
    deadline=min(time.monotonic()+max_time, global_deadline)
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
        it += 1
        inner_budget = int(min(120, max(5, (deadline - time.monotonic())/3)))
        if inner_budget < 5: break
        g=balance_inner(best_f, inner_budget, deadline, warm_g=warm)
        if g is None: break
        gmax, Sg = normalize_max_and_sum(g)
        if gmax is None: break
        improved=False
        for t in (1.0,0.8,0.6,0.45,0.3,0.18,0.08):
             cand= (1-t)*best_f + t*gmax
             c, Sc = normalize_max_and_sum(cand)
             if c is not None and Sc > best_S + 1e-10:
                 best_f=c; best_S=Sc; improved=True
        if not improved:
            # If gmax itself similar? maybe break after few
            if it > 2: break
        warm = gmax if gmax is not None else g
    b=best_f/best_S
    return b
```
If no improvement, break after 2. But maybe need multiple inner to improve? If gmax line search no improvement, likely local. Break. If improvement, continue.

`direct_lbfgs`:
```
def direct_lbfgs(a, max_time, global_deadline):
   a=... sum1
   score=score_from_a(a); best=a
   n=len(a); N=next_pow2(3*n-2); set globals; G_DEADLINE=deadline
   v=np.log(a+1e-300)
   factors=[0.3,0.15,0.07,0.03,0.015,0.008]
   base=maxiter_for(n, max_time*len(factors))
   for factor in factors:
      if time >= deadline-1: break
      M=score/(2*n) if score>0 else 1/(2*n)
      tau=factor*max(M,1e-12)
      G_ORIG_TAU=tau; G_DEADLINE=deadline
      maxiter=max(20, base//len(factors))
      try: res=minimize(orig_obj, v, jac=orig_grad, ...)
      v=res.x
      a=softmax(v); sc=score_from_a(a)
      if sc<score: score=sc; best=a
   return best
```

def row_solve_refine(a, max_time, global_deadline):

   best_f=nm; best_S=S; deadline...

`polish_subgradient`:
```
def polish_subgradient(a, max_time, global_deadline):
   a=... sum1; best=a; best_sc=score_from_a(a); deadline...
   n=len(a); N=next_pow2(3*n-2); S=2*n-2; idx=S-np.arange(n)
   step=0.08; top=min(300,n)
   while time < deadline-2:
      b=conv_square(best); M=b.max(); if M<=0: break
      if top < len(b): idx_b=np.argpartition(b, -top)[-top:]
      else: idx_b=np.arange(len(b))
      w=np.zeros(len(b)); w[idx_b]=1.0; sw=w.sum(); w/=sw; w=0.5*(w+w[::-1]); sw=w.sum(); if sw>0: w/=sw
      fa=np.fft.rfft(best,N); wh=np.fft.rfft(np.flip(w),N)
      c=np.fft.irfft(fa*wh,N); h=c[idx]
      grad=2*h; dot=np.dot(best,grad); dir=best*(grad-dot)
      v=np.log(best+1e-300)
      improved=False
      for alpha in (step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.12, step*0.05):
          vv=v-alpha*dir
          aa=softmax(vv)
          sc=score_from_a(aa)
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
              best=aa; best_sc=sc; improved=True
              break
      if not improved:
          step*=0.75
      else:
          step=min(0.3, step*1.03)
      if step < 1e-5: break
   return best
```

           if b.size>0: consider_sequence(b, tracker)
    return finalize(tracker)

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative step-function sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The search combines several ideas:
  1. Start from supplied state-of-art / simple analytic profiles.
  2. Alternate between a current sequence f and the optimal g for the
     convex LP subproblem  min_g max_k (f * g)_k  over the simplex.
     This is implemented with LSE-smoothed L-BFGS in log-space.
  3. A KKT-inspired row response: choose active lag weights lambda from
     f*f, then solve approximately T_lambda x = 1 where
     (T_lambda x)_i = sum_j lambda_{i+j} x_j.
  4. Final polishing with full-variable smoothed L-BFGS and exact
     subgradient steps.
All heavy convolutions and gradients are computed with FFTs.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# ----------------------------- global state -----------------------------
G_INNER_N = 0
G_INNER_NFFT = 0
G_INNER_IDX = None
G_INNER_FHAT = None
G_INNER_TAU = 1.0
G_INNER_S = 0

G_ORIG_N = 0
G_ORIG_NFFT = 0
G_ORIG_IDX = None
G_ORIG_S = 0
G_ORIG_TAU = 1.0

G_DEADLINE = 0.0


def stop_callback(_):
    if time.monotonic() > G_DEADLINE:
        return True
    return False


# ----------------------------- basic helpers ----------------------------
def softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-1e6)
    m = float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    return e / s


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def conv_square(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 800:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N, n=2 * n - 1)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c


def score_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    b = conv_square(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    M = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def normalize_max_and_sum(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None, 0.0
    a = a / s
    b = conv_square(a)
    M = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
        return a.copy(), float(s)
    scale = float(np.sqrt(M))
    a = a / scale
    return a.copy(), float(a.sum())


def profile_B(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(1, n):
        a[i] = a[i - 1] * (2 * i - 1) / (2 * i)
    return a


def two_endpoint_profile(n, alpha=0.5):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(i + 1.0, -alpha) + np.power(float(n) - i, -alpha)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        a = a / s
    return a


def flat_root_profile(n, L):
    """
    First n coefficients of a nonnegative polynomial G with
    G(0)=L and coefficients of G^2 equal to 1 for lags 1..n-1
    whenever the recursion stays nonnegative.  This is used as a
    constructive diverse starting profile.
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    L = float(max(1e-8, L))
    g[0] = L
    for k in range(1, n):
        if k > 1:
            s = float(np.dot(g[1:k], g[k - 1:0:-1]))
        else:
            s = 0.0
        val = (1.0 - s) / (2.0 * L)
        if not np.isfinite(val) or val < 0.0:
            val = 0.0
        g[k] = val
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        g = g / s
    return g


def interp_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, a).astype(np.float64)


def perturb_sequence(a, sigma=0.1, seed=123):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = a / s
    v = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    v = v + rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=v.size)
    return softmax(v)


def trim_edges(a, eps):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or a.size <= 10:
        if s > 0.0:
            return a / s
        return a
    a = a / s
    mx = float(a.max())
    thresh = max(float(eps) * mx, 10.0 * np.finfo(np.float64).tiny)
    l, r = 0, a.size - 1
    while l < r and a[l] < thresh:
        l += 1
    while r > l and a[r] < thresh:
        r -= 1
    b = a[l:r + 1]
    s2 = float(b.sum())
    if s2 > 0.0:
        b = b / s2
    return b


def consider_sequence(seq, tracker):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return
    a = a / s
    internal = score_from_a(a)
    if not np.isfinite(internal):
        return
    cand_score = internal
    # Verify promising near-target candidates with the official evaluator.
    if internal < 1.55 and tracker[2] < 30 and n <= 12000:
        try:
            ex = evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
        except Exception:
            ex = internal
        tracker[2] += 1
        if np.isfinite(ex):
            cand_score = float(ex)
    if np.isfinite(cand_score) and cand_score < tracker[0] - 1e-12:
        tracker[0] = float(cand_score)
        tracker[1] = a.copy()


def finalize_candidates(tracker):
    best = tracker[1]
    cands = []
    if best is not None:
        a = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64)
        a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            cands.append(a / s)
        cands.append(trim_edges(best, 1e-7))
        cands.append(trim_edges(best, 1e-6))
        cands.append(trim_edges(best, 1e-5))
        b = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        b = b + 1e-15
        s2 = float(b.sum())
        if s2 > 0.0:
            cands.append(b / s2)
    if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
        try:
            h = np.asarray(globals()["height_sequence_1"], dtype=np.float64)
            h = h[np.isfinite(h)]
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            sh = float(h.sum())
            if sh > 0.0:
                cands.append(h / sh)
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    for c in cands:
        if c is None:
            continue
        arr = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s < 1e-3 or arr.size == 0:
            continue
        arr = arr / s
        try:
            sc = evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist())
        except Exception:
            sc = score_from_a(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = arr

    if best_seq is None:
        if best is not None:
            best_seq = np.maximum(np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
        else:
            best_seq = profile_B(256)
    best_seq = best_seq[np.isfinite(best_seq)]
    best_seq = np.maximum(best_seq, 0.0)
    s = float(best_seq.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return [0.01]
    best_seq = best_seq / s
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]


def maxiter_for(n, budget):
    if n <= 2048:
        base = 900
    elif n <= 4096:
        base = 650
    elif n <= 6000:
        base = 480
    elif n <= 8192:
        base = 340
    elif n <= 12000:
        base = 230
    else:
        base = 160
    try:
        b = float(budget)
    except Exception:
        b = 60.0
    if not np.isfinite(b) or b <= 0.0:
        b = 60.0
    scale = min(1.0, max(0.25, b / 60.0))
    return max(20, int(base * scale))


# ----------------------------- LF operators -----------------------------
def apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat, N, idx, x):
    xh = np.fft.rfft(x, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(w_rev_hat * xh, N)
    return c[idx].copy()


def make_lambda_soft(b, tau, sym=True):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if M <= 0.0 or tau <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
    u = (b - M) / tau
    u = np.clip(u, -100.0, 0.0)
    w = np.exp(u)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros_like(b, dtype=np.float64)
    w = w / s
    if sym:
        w = 0.5 * (w + w[::-1])
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            w = w / s
    return w


def solve_T_lambda_one(w, n, iters=45, mu_frac=1e-5):
    """
    Solve approximately min ||T_lambda x - T_lambda 1|| with T_lambda
    symmetric Hankel (T_lambda x)_i = sum_j lambda_{i+j} x_j, then return
    a nonnegative normalized vector.
    """
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    while w.size < L:
        w = np.concatenate((w, np.zeros(L - w.size, dtype=np.float64)))
    if w.size > L:
        w = w[:L]
    S = L - 1
    idx = S - np.arange(n)
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    w_rev_hat = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(w), N)

    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat, N, idx, ones)
    normb = float(np.max(np.abs(b)))
    if normb <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(normb):
        return ones / float(n)

    mu = max(1e-12, float(mu_frac) * normb)
    x = ones / float(n)

    y = apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat, N, idx, x)
    Ty = apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat, N, idx, y)
    r = b - (Ty + mu * x)
    p = r.copy()
    rsold = float(np.dot(r, r))

    for _ in range(int(max(1, iters))):
        y = apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat, N, idx, p)
        Ty = apply_T_generic(w_rev_hat, N, idx, y)
        Ap = Ty + mu * p
        pAp = float(np.dot(p, Ap))
        if not np.isfinite(pAp) or pAp <= 1e-300:
            break
        alpha = rsold / pAp
        x = x + alpha * p
        r = r - alpha * Ap
        rsnew = float(np.dot(r, r))
        if not np.isfinite(rsnew) or rsnew < 1e-18 * normb * normb:
            break
        if rsold <= 1e-300:
            break
        p = r + (rsnew / rsold) * p
        rsold = rsnew

    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return ones / float(n)
    return x / s


# --------------------- inner convex balance subproblem --------------------
def inner_obj(v):
    try:
        if time.monotonic() > G_DEADLINE:
            return float("inf")
        n = G_INNER_N
        if n <= 0 or G_INNER_FHAT is None:
            return float("inf")
        g = softmax(v)
        N = G_INNER_NFFT
        gh = np.fft.rfft(g, N)
        b = np.fft.irfft(G_INNER_FHAT * gh, N, n=2 * n - 1)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        M = float(b.max())
        tau = G_INNER_TAU
        if tau <= 1e-300 or M <= 0.0:
            return M
        u = (b - M) / tau
        u = np.clip(u, -100.0, 0.0)
        ew = np.exp(u)
        s = float(ew.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return M
        return float(M + tau * np.log(s))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def inner_grad(v):
    try:
        if time.monotonic() > G_DEADLINE:
            return np.zeros(np.asarray(v).size, dtype=np.float64)
        n = G_INNER_N
        if n <= 0 or G_INNER_FHAT is None:
            return np.zeros(np.asarray(v).size, dtype=np.float64)
        g = softmax(v)
        N = G_INNER_NFFT
        gh = np.fft.rfft(g, N)
        b = np.fft.irfft(G_INNER_FHAT * gh, N, n=2 * n - 1)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        M = float(b.max())
        tau = G_INNER_TAU
        if tau <= 1e-300 or M <= 0.0:
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        u = (b - M) / tau
        u = np.clip(u, -100.0, 0.0)
        ew = np.exp(u)
        s = float(ew.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w = ew / s
        # (T_w f)_i = sum_k w_k f_{k-i}; use reverse-convolution trick.
        wr = np.flip(w)
        wr_hat = np.fft.rfft(wr, N)
        h = np.fft.irfft(G_INNER_FHAT * wr_hat, N)
        h = h[G_INNER_IDX].copy()
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        dot = float(np.dot(g, h))
        grad = g * (h - dot)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return grad
    except Exception:
        return np.zeros(np.asarray(v).size, dtype=np.float64)


def inner_exact_global(g):
    try:
        n = G_INNER_N
        if n <= 0 or G_INNER_FHAT is None:
            return float("inf")
        gh = np.fft.rfft(g, G_INNER_NFFT)
        b = np.fft.irfft(G_INNER_FHAT * gh, G_INNER_NFFT, n=2 * n - 1)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        return float(b.max())
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def balance_inner(f, max_time, global_deadline, warm_g=None):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    f = f[np.isfinite(f)]
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    s = float(f.sum())
    n = f.size
    if s <= 1e-12 or n < 2:
        return None

    # f is expected max-normalized, but normalize once to be safe.
    fn, _ = normalize_max_and_sum(f)
    if fn is None:
        return None
    f = fn

    deadline = min(time.monotonic() + float(max_time), float(global_deadline))
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return f / float(f.sum())

    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    S = 2 * n - 2
    idx = S - np.arange(n)
    fh = np.fft.rfft(f, N)

    G_INNER_N = n
    G_INNER_NFFT = N
    G_INNER_IDX = idx
    G_INNER_FHAT = fh
    G_INNER_S = S

    if warm_g is not None and np.asarray(warm_g).size == n:
        g = np.asarray(warm_g, dtype=np.float64)
        g = g[np.isfinite(g)]
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        sg = float(g.sum())
        if sg <= 0.0:
            g = f / float(f.sum())
        else:
            g = g / sg
    else:
        sf = float(f.sum())
        g = f / sf if sf > 0.0 else np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    v = np.log(g + 1e-300)
    best_g = g.copy()
    best_val = inner_exact_global(best_g)

    factors = [0.30, 0.15, 0.07, 0.03, 0.015]
    total_iters = maxiter_for(n, max_time)
    per = max(20, total_iters // len(factors))

    for factor in factors:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 0.5:
            break
        if best_val <= 0.0:
            break
        G_INNER_TAU = float(factor) * max(best_val, 1e-12)
        G_DEADLINE = deadline
        try:
            res = minimize(
                inner_obj,
                v,
                jac=inner_grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": per,
                    "maxfun": per * 4,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxls": 10,
                    "disp": False,
                },
                callback=stop_callback,
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                v = res.x
        except Exception:
            pass

        g2 = softmax(v)
        val2 = inner_exact_global(g2)
        if np.isfinite(val2) and val2 < best_val:
            best_val = val2
            best_g = g2.copy()

    return best_g


def outer_balance(a, max_time, global_deadline):
    f0, S0 = normalize_max_and_sum(a)
    if f0 is None:
        return None
    best_f = f0.copy()
    best_S = float(S0)
    warm = None
    deadline = min(time.monotonic() + float(max_time), float(global_deadline))
    rounds = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        rounds += 1
        inner_budget = int(min(120.0, max(5.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) / 3.0)))
        if inner_budget < 5:
            break
        g = balance_inner(best_f, inner_budget, deadline, warm_g=warm)
        if g is None:
            break
        gmax, _ = normalize_max_and_sum(g)
        if gmax is None:
            break

        improved = False
        for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.45, 0.30, 0.18, 0.08):
            cand = (1.0 - t) * best_f + t * gmax
            c, Sc = normalize_max_and_sum(cand)
            if c is not None and Sc > best_S + 1e-10:
                best_f = c
                best_S = Sc
                improved = True

        if not improved and rounds > 2:
            break
        warm = g

    out = best_f / best_S if best_S > 0.0 else None
    return out


# --------------------------- original smooth L-BFGS ----------------------
def orig_obj(v):
    try:
        if time.monotonic() > G_DEADLINE:
            return float("inf")
        n = G_ORIG_N
        if n <= 0:
            return float("inf")
        a = softmax(v)
        N = G_ORIG_NFFT
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N, n=2 * n - 1)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        M = float(b.max())
        tau = G_ORIG_TAU
        if tau <= 1e-300 or M <= 0.0:
            return M
        u = (b - M) / tau
        u = np.clip(u, -100.0, 0.0)
        ew = np.exp(u)
        s = float(ew.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return M
        return float(M + tau * np.log(s))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def orig_grad(v):
    try:
        if time.monotonic() > G_DEADLINE:
            return np.zeros(np.asarray(v).size, dtype=np.float64)
        n = G_ORIG_N
        if n <= 0:
            return np.zeros(np.asarray(v).size, dtype=np.float64)
        a = softmax(v)
        N = G_ORIG_NFFT
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N, n=2 * n - 1)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        M = float(b.max())
        tau = G_ORIG_TAU
        if tau <= 1e-300 or M <= 0.0:
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        u = (b - M) / tau
        u = np.clip(u, -100.0, 0.0)
        ew = np.exp(u)
        s = float(ew.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w = ew / s
        w = 0.5 * (w + w[::-1])
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws > 0.0:
            w = w / ws
        wr = np.flip(w)
        wr_hat = np.fft.rfft(wr, N)
        h = np.fft.irfft(fa * wr_hat, N)
        h = h[G_ORIG_IDX].copy()
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        grad_a = 2.0 * h
        dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
        grad = a * (grad_a - dot)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return grad
    except Exception:
        return np.zeros(np.asarray(v).size, dtype=np.float64)


def direct_lbfgs(a, max_time, global_deadline):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if s <= 1e-12 or n < 2:
        return a
    a = a / s
    score = score_from_a(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return a
    best = a.copy()

    deadline = min(time.monotonic() + float(max_time), float(global_deadline))
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    S = 2 * n - 2
    idx = S - np.arange(n)
    G_ORIG_N = n
    G_ORIG_NFFT = N
    G_ORIG_IDX = idx
    G_ORIG_S = S
    G_DEADLINE = deadline

    v = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    factors = [0.30, 0.15, 0.07, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008]
    total_iters = maxiter_for(n, max_time)
    per = max(20, total_iters // len(factors))

    for factor in factors:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
            break
        M = score / (2.0 * n) if score > 0.0 else 1.0 / (2.0 * n)
        G_ORIG_TAU = float(factor) * max(M, 1e-12)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                orig_obj,
                v,
                jac=orig_grad,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": per,
                    "maxfun": per * 4,
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxls": 10,
                    "disp": False,
                },
                callback=stop_callback,
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                v = res.x
        except Exception:
            pass

        a2 = softmax(v)
        sc2 = score_from_a(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < score:
            score = sc2
            best = a2.copy()

    return best


# ------------------------------ row-response refine ----------------------
def row_solve_refine(a, max_time, global_deadline):
    f0, S0 = normalize_max_and_sum(a)
    if f0 is None:
        return None
    best_f = f0.copy()
    best_S = float(S0)
    deadline = min(time.monotonic() + float(max_time), float(global_deadline))
    n = f0.size
    factors = [0.35, 0.20, 0.10, 0.05]
    iters = 50 if n <= 6000 else 35 if n <= 10000 else 25

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        improved = False
        for factor in factors:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
                break
            b = conv_square(best_f)
            M = float(b.max())
            if M <= 0.0:
                break
            w = make_lambda_soft(b, float(factor) * M, sym=True)
            cand = solve_T_lambda_one(w, n, iters=iters, mu_frac=1e-5)
            cmax, _ = normalize_max_and_sum(cand)
            if cmax is None:
                continue
            for t in (1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3, 0.15):
                cc = (1.0 - t) * best_f + t * cmax
                ccn, Sc = normalize_max_and_sum(cc)
                if ccn is not None and Sc > best_S + 1e-10:
                    best_f = ccn
                    best_S = Sc
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break
        if not improved:
            break

    return best_f / best_S if best_S > 0.0 else None


# ----------------------------- exact subgradient polish ------------------
def polish_subgradient(a, max_time, global_deadline):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if s <= 1e-12 or n < 2:
        return a
    best = a / s
    best_score = score_from_a(best)
    deadline = min(time.monotonic() + float(max_time), float(global_deadline))
    N = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    S = 2 * n - 2
    idx = S - np.arange(n)
    step = 0.08
    top = min(320, n)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        b = conv_square(best)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M <= 0.0:
            break
        if top < b.size:
            act = np.argpartition(b, -top)[-top:]
        else:
            act = np.arange(b.size)
        w = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        w[act] = 1.0
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 0.0:
            break
        w = w / ws
        w = 0.5 * (w + w[::-1])
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 0.0:
            break
        w = w / ws

        fa = np.fft.rfft(best, N)
        wh = np.fft.rfft(np.flip(w), N)
        h = np.fft.irfft(fa * wh, N)
        h = h[idx].copy()
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        grad = 2.0 * h
        dot = float(np.dot(best, grad))
        direction = best * (grad - dot)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(direction)):
            break
        v = np.log(best + 1e-300)
        improved = False
        for alpha in (step, 0.5 * step, 0.25 * step, 0.12 * step, 0.05 * step):
            v2 = v - alpha * direction
            a2 = softmax(v2)
            sc2 = score_from_a(a2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-13:
                best = a2
                best_score = sc2
                improved = True
                break
        if not improved:
            step *= 0.75
        else:
            step = min(0.30, step * 1.03)
        if step < 1e-5:
            break
    return best


# ----------------------------- run one start -----------------------------
def run_start(a, budget, tracker, global_deadline, allow_direct=True):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if s <= 1e-12 or n < 8 or n > 15000:
        return
    a = a / s

    rem = float(global_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem < 40.0:
        return

    outer_budget = int(min(0.62 * float(budget), max(20.0, 0.45 * rem)))
    if outer_budget >= 20:
        out = outer_balance(a, outer_budget, global_deadline)
        if out is not None:
            consider_sequence(out, tracker)
            if tracker[1] is not None:
                a = tracker[1].copy()

    rem = float(global_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if len(a) <= 15000:
        row_budget = int(min(0.14 * float(budget), 60.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * rem)))
        if row_budget >= 18:
            out = row_solve_refine(a, row_budget, global_deadline)
            if out is not None:
                consider_sequence(out, tracker)
                if tracker[1] is not None:
                    a = tracker[1].copy()

    if allow_direct:
        rem = float(global_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if len(a) <= 12000:
            direct_budget = int(min(0.25 * float(budget), 300.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * rem)))
            if direct_budget >= 35:
                out = direct_lbfgs(a, direct_budget, global_deadline)
                consider_sequence(out, tracker)
                if tracker[1] is not None:
                    a = tracker[1].copy()

    rem = float(global_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if len(a) <= 15000:
        pol_budget = int(min(0.12 * float(budget), 70.0, max(0.0, rem)))
        if pol_budget >= 10:
            out = polish_subgradient(a, pol_budget, global_deadline)
            consider_sequence(out, tracker)


# ------------------------------- main entry ------------------------------
def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(budget, 2400.0)

    # Leave a safety margin for the final best evaluation.
    global_deadline = time.monotonic() + budget - 90.0
    tracker = [float("inf"), None, 0]

    # Baseline: supplied state-of-art if present.
    if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
        try:
            a0 = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
            consider_sequence(a0, tracker)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Fallback analytic start.
    if tracker[1] is None:
        n_base = 4096
        consider_sequence(profile_B(n_base), tracker)

    n_base = len(tracker[1]) if tracker[1] is not None else 4096
    print(f"initial best score={tracker[0]:.9f}, n={n_base}")

    plans = []
    rem = float(global_deadline - time.monotonic())

    if rem > 420 and tracker[1] is not None:
        plans.append(("base", tracker[1].copy(), 520, True))
    if rem > 760:
        plans.append(("B", profile_B(n_base), 260, True))
    if n_base <= 6000 and rem > 960:
        plans.append(("flat085", flat_root_profile(n_base, 0.85), 220, True))
    if n_base <= 6000 and rem > 1120:
        plans.append(("flat080", flat_root_profile(n_base, 0.80), 190, False))
    if rem > 1280:
        plans.append(("twoeps", two_endpoint_profile(n_base, 0.5), 150, False))
    if rem > 1440 and tracker[1] is not None:
        plans.append(("perturb", perturb_sequence(tracker[1], 0.12, 123), 200, False))

    # Test other lengths, using the current best profile when available.
    other_lengths = []
    if n_base <= 8192:
        other_lengths.append(8192)
    if n_base <= 12288:
        other_lengths.append(12288)

    for n in other_lengths:
        if n == n_base or n < 1024 or n > 14000:
            continue
        if time.monotonic() > global_deadline - 360.0:
            break
        if tracker[1] is not None:
            plans.append((f"interp{n}", interp_sequence(tracker[1], n), 230, False))
        plans.append((f"B{n}", profile_B(n), 230, False))

    for label, seq, bud, allowd in plans:
        if seq is None:
            continue
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
        arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12 or arr.size < 8 or arr.size > 15000:
            continue
        arr = arr / s
        rem = float(global_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 120.0:
            break
        use_budget = int(min(float(bud), 0.62 * rem))
        if use_budget < 30:
            continue
        print(f"phase {label} n={arr.size} budget={use_budget}")
        run_start(arr, use_budget, tracker, global_deadline, allow_direct=allowd)
        print(f"after {label}: best={tracker[0]:.9f}")

    # Final polish on the global best.
    if tracker[1] is not None and time.monotonic() < global_deadline:
        a = tracker[1].copy()
        rem = float(global_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if len(a) <= 12000 and rem > 90:
            direct_budget = int(min(300.0, 0.55 * rem))
            if direct_budget > 45:
                out = direct_lbfgs(a, direct_budget, global_deadline)
                consider_sequence(out, tracker)
                a = tracker[1]

        rem = float(global_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if len(a) <= 15000 and rem > 40:
            row_budget = int(min(120.0, 0.55 * rem))
            if row_budget > 20:
                out = row_solve_refine(a, row_budget, global_deadline)
                consider_sequence(out, tracker)
                a = tracker[1]

        rem = float(global_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if len(a) <= 15000 and rem > 20:
            pol_budget = int(min(120.0, rem))
            if pol_budget > 10:
                out = polish_subgradient(a, pol_budget, global_deadline)
                consider_sequence(out, tracker)

        for eps in (1e-7, 1e-6, 1e-5):
            out = trim_edges(tracker[1], eps)
            if out is not None and out.size > 0:
                consider_sequence(out, tracker)

    print(f"pre-final best internal={tracker[0]:.9f}")
    return finalize_candidates(tracker)
```
